CODING — DEEP MASTER CORPUS: 500 EXAMPLES
=============================================

This corpus contains exactly 500 compact, cross-language programming examples.

LANGUAGES
---------
Python      63
JavaScript  63
C           63
C++         63
C#          62
Java        62
PHP         62
Kotlin      62
TOTAL       500

COVERAGE
--------
Every language is covered across 63 syntax/data-structure categories:
primitive values, operators, control flow, functions, strings, arrays/lists,
tuples/records, sets, maps/dictionaries, stacks, queues/deques, linked lists,
trees, heaps/priority queues, graphs, hashing, sorting/searching, recursion,
exceptions, files/serialization, generics/templates, classes, inheritance,
interfaces, polymorphism, encapsulation, functional programming, concurrency,
HTTP/networking, databases, testing, references/pointers, modules, reflection,
regex, iterators/generators, advanced syntax, collection pipelines, enums,
nullable values, pattern matching, CLI arguments, environment variables, time,
logging, configuration, dependency injection, API validation, caching,
pagination, authentication, authorization, transactions, data validation,
serialization formats, CSV, binary data, bit operations, case studies,
algorithms, production hardening, performance and debugging.

The user's supplied reference also mentioned Django, Flask, Tkinter, Nuitka,
Requests, NumPy, Pandas, Matplotlib, TensorFlow/Keras, Node.js, Express,
AMQPlib/RabbitMQ, React, jQuery, CSS/SCSS, Three.js, GSAP, Anime.js, MSVC/MFC,
Jakarta EE, Spring Boot, WordPress, WooCommerce and Shopify Liquid. These are
ecosystem technologies rather than additional general-purpose languages, so
the 500 core examples are organized around the eight programming languages.

IMPORTANT
---------
These are compact training examples, not substitutes for language
specifications. External-library examples are intentionally marked or kept
minimal. Equivalent syntax across languages does not imply equivalent memory,
type, nullability, ownership or concurrency semantics.

Map              | dict | Map/Object | custom hash table | map/unordered_map | Dictionary | Map/HashMap | associative array | Map
Stack            | list/deque | Array | array+top | stack/vector | Stack | Deque | array/SplStack | ArrayDeque
Queue            | deque | Array | ring buffer | queue/deque | Queue | Queue/ArrayDeque | SplQueue/array | ArrayDeque
Linked List      | custom nodes | custom nodes | struct pointers | list/custom nodes | LinkedList/custom | LinkedList/custom | objects | custom nodes
Tree             | custom nodes | custom nodes | struct pointers | custom nodes | custom classes | custom classes | objects | data classes
Heap             | heapq | custom heap | custom heap | priority_queue | PriorityQueue | PriorityQueue | SplPriorityQueue | PriorityQueue
Graph            | dict/list | object/Map | matrix/list | vector/map | Dictionary/List | Map/List | arrays | Map/List

CASE STUDIES
-----------
1. E-commerce: collections, carts, queues, validation, caching and transactions.
2. Payments: authentication, authorization, exceptions, transaction boundaries and audit logging.
3. REST APIs: HTTP, JSON, validation, pagination, caching and database access.
4. Sensor systems: arrays, pointers/references, queues, thresholds and diagnostics.
5. ETL: files, CSV, serialization, maps, sorting, validation and aggregation.
6. Graph systems: adjacency lists, BFS/DFS concepts, sets, queues and hashing.
7. Scheduling: heaps, priority queues, sorting and concurrency.
8. Enterprise services: classes, interfaces, dependency injection, testing and persistence.
9. Mobile applications: Kotlin collections, null safety, coroutines and serialization.
10. CMS/e-commerce: PHP arrays, classes, database access and framework architecture.

ECOSYSTEM MAP FROM THE SUPPLIED REFERENCE
------------------------------------------
Python: Django, Flask, Tkinter, Nuitka, Requests, NumPy, Pandas, Matplotlib, TensorFlow/Keras.
JavaScript: Node.js, Express.js, AMQPlib/RabbitMQ, React, jQuery, Three.js, GSAP, Anime.js.
C/C++: MSVC/MFC and native systems/graphics tooling.
Java: Jakarta EE/J2EE and Spring Boot.
PHP: WordPress and WooCommerce.
Template/UI: Shopify Liquid and CSS/SCSS.

CORPUS TRAINING TASKS
---------------------
• Predict output.
• Explain every token.
• Identify the data structure.
• Convert the example between languages.
• Add invalid-input handling.
• Add unit and integration tests.
• Estimate time and space complexity.
• Identify memory ownership/reference behavior.
• Refactor into production-quality code.
• Turn each mini example into a complete case-study application.
